1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWe present DeepSeek-V3, a strong Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model with 671B total parameters with 37B activated for each token.\nTo achieve efficient inference and cost-effective training, DeepSeek-V3 adopts Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) and DeepSeekMoE architectures, which were thoroughly validated in DeepSeek-V2.\nFurthermore, DeepSeek-V3 pioneers an auxiliary-loss-free strategy for load balancing and sets a multi-token prediction training objective for stronger performance.\nWe pre-train DeepSeek-V3 on 14.8 trillion diverse and high-quality tokens, followed by Supervised Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning stages to fully harness its capabilities.\nComprehensive evaluations reveal that DeepSeek-V3 outperforms other open-source models and achieves performance comparable to leading closed-source models.\nDespite its excellent performance, DeepSeek-V3 requires only 2.788M H800 GPU hours for its full training.\nIn addition, its training process is remarkably stable.\nThroughout the entire training process, we did not experience any irrecoverable loss spikes or perform any rollbacks.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n  \u003ca target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3/blob/main/figures/benchmark.png\"\u003e\u003cimg width=\"80%\" src=\"/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3/raw/main/figures/benchmark.png\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2. Model Summary\" href=\"#2-model-summary\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eArchitecture: Innovative Load Balancing Strategy and Training Objective\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOn top of the efficient architecture of DeepSeek-V2, we pioneer an auxiliary-loss-free strategy for load balancing, which minimizes the performance degradation that arises from encouraging load balancing.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWe investigate a Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) objective and prove it beneficial to model performance.\nIt can also be used for speculative decoding for inference acceleration.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePre-Training: Towards Ultimate Training Efficiency\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWe design an FP8 mixed precision training framework and, for the first time, validate the feasibility and effectiveness of FP8 training on an extremely large-scale model.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThrough co-design of algorithms, frameworks, and hardware, we overcome the communication bottleneck in cross-node MoE training, nearly achieving full computation-communication overlap.\u003cbr\u003e\nThis significantly enhances our training efficiency and reduces the training costs, enabling us to further scale up the model size without additional overhead.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAt an economical cost of only 2.664M H800 GPU hours, we complete the pre-training of DeepSeek-V3 on 14.8T tokens, producing the currently strongest open-source base model. The subsequent training stages after pre-training require only 0.1M GPU hours.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePost-Training: Knowledge Distillation from DeepSeek-R1\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWe introduce an innovative methodology to distill reasoning capabilities from the long-Chain-of-Thought (CoT) model, specifically from one of the DeepSeek R1 series models, into standard LLMs, particularly DeepSeek-V3. Our pipeline elegantly incorporates the verification and reflection patterns of R1 into DeepSeek-V3 and notably improves its reasoning performance. Meanwhile, we also maintain a control over the output style and length of DeepSeek-V3.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3. aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDeepSeek-V3 can be deployed locally using the following hardware and open-source community software:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDeepSeek-Infer Demo\u003c/strong\u003e: We provide a simple and lightweight demo for FP8 and BF16 inference.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSGLang\u003c/strong\u003e: Fully support the DeepSeek-V3 model in both BF16 and FP8 inference modes, with Multi-Token Prediction \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/issues/2591\" data-hovercard-type=\"issue\" data-hovercard-url=\"/sgl-project/sglang/issues/2591/hovercard\"\u003ecoming soon\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLMDeploy\u003c/strong\u003e: Enables efficient FP8 and BF16 inference for local and cloud deployment.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTensorRT-LLM\u003c/strong\u003e: Currently supports BF16 inference and INT4/8 quantization, with FP8 support coming soon.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003evLLM\u003c/strong\u003e: Support DeepSeek-V3 model with FP8 and BF16 modes for tensor parallelism and pipeline parallelism.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLightLLM\u003c/strong\u003e: Supports efficient single-node or multi-node deployment for FP8 and BF16.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAMD GPU\u003c/strong\u003e: Enables running the DeepSeek-V3 model on AMD GPUs via SGLang in both BF16 and FP8 modes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHuawei Ascend NPU\u003c/strong\u003e: Supports running DeepSeek-V3 on Huawei Ascend devices in both INT8 and BF16.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSince FP8 training is natively adopted in our framework, we only provide FP8 weights. <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Introduction</h2><a id="user-content-1-introduction" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Introduction" href="#1-introduction"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We present DeepSeek-V3, a strong Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model with 671B total parameters with 37B activated for each token.
To achieve efficient inference and cost-effective training, DeepSeek-V3 adopts Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) and DeepSeekMoE architectures, which were thoroughly validated in DeepSeek-V2.
Furthermore, DeepSeek-V3 pioneers an auxiliary-loss-free strategy for load balancing and sets a multi-token prediction training objective for stronger performance.
We pre-train DeepSeek-V3 on 14.8 trillion diverse and high-quality tokens, followed by Supervised Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning stages to fully harness its capabilities.
Comprehensive evaluations reveal that DeepSeek-V3 outperforms other open-source models and achieves performance comparable to leading closed-source models.
Despite its excellent performance, DeepSeek-V3 requires only 2.788M H800 GPU hours for its full training.
In addition, its training process is remarkably stable.
Throughout the entire training process, we did not experience any irrecoverable loss spikes or perform any rollbacks.</p>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3/blob/main/figures/benchmark.png"><img width="80%" src="/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3/raw/main/figures/benchmark.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Model Summary</h2><a id="user-content-2-model-summary" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Model Summary" href="#2-model-summary"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<hr>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Architecture: Innovative Load Balancing Strategy and Training Objective</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>On top of the efficient architecture of DeepSeek-V2, we pioneer an auxiliary-loss-free strategy for load balancing, which minimizes the performance degradation that arises from encouraging load balancing.</li>
<li>We investigate a Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) objective and prove it beneficial to model performance.
It can also be used for speculative decoding for inference acceleration.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Pre-Training: Towards Ultimate Training Efficiency</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>We design an FP8 mixed precision training framework and, for the first time, validate the feasibility and effectiveness of FP8 training on an extremely large-scale model.</li>
<li>Through co-design of algorithms, frameworks, and hardware, we overcome the communication bottleneck in cross-node MoE training, nearly achieving full computation-communication overlap.<br>
This significantly enhances our training efficiency and reduces the training costs, enabling us to further scale up the model size without additional overhead.</li>
<li>At an economical cost of only 2.664M H800 GPU hours, we complete the pre-training of DeepSeek-V3 on 14.8T tokens, producing the currently strongest open-source base model. The subsequent training stages after pre-training require only 0.1M GPU hours.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Post-Training: Knowledge Distillation from DeepSeek-R1</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>We introduce an innovative methodology to distill reasoning capabilities from the long-Chain-of-Thought (CoT) model, specifically from one of the DeepSeek R1 series models, into standard LLMs, particularly DeepSeek-V3. Our pipeline elegantly incorporates the verification and reflection patterns of R1 into DeepSeek-V3 and notably improves its reasoning performance. Meanwhile, we also maintain a control over the output style and length of DeepSeek-V3.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Model Downloads</h2><a id="user-content-3-model-downloads" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. <div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">The total size of DeepSeek-V3 models on Hugging Face is 685B, which includes 671B of the Main Model weights and 14B of the Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) Module weights.</p>
</div>
<p dir="auto">To ensure optimal performance and flexibility, we have partnered with open-source communities and hardware vendors to provide multiple ways to run the model locally. For step-by-step guidance, check out Section 6: <a href="#6-how-to-run-locally">How_to Run_Locally</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">For developers looking to dive deeper, we recommend exploring <a href="/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3/blob/main/README_WEIGHTS.md">README_WEIGHTS.md</a> for details on the Main Model weights and the Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) Modules. Please note that MTP support is currently under active development within the community, and we welcome your contributions and feedback.</p>

<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">Best results are shown in bold. Scores with a gap not exceeding 0.3 are considered to be at the same level. DeepSeek-V3 achieves the best performance on most benchmarks, especially on math and code tasks.
For more evaluation details, please check our paper.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">5. Chat Website &amp; API Platform</h2><a id="user-content-5-chat-website--api-platform" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 5. Chat Website &amp; API Platform" href="#5-chat-website--api-platform"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can chat with DeepSeek-V3 on DeepSeek's official website: <a href="https://chat.deepseek.com/sign_in" rel="nofollow">chat.deepseek.com</a></p>
<p dir="auto">We also provide OpenAI-Compatible API at DeepSeek Platform: <a href="https://platform.deepseek.com/" rel="nofollow">platform.deepseek.com</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6. How to Run Locally</h2><a id="user-content-6-how-to-run-locally" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6. How to Run Locally" href="#6-how-to-run-locally"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">DeepSeek-V3 can be deployed locally using the following hardware and open-source community software:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>DeepSeek-Infer Demo</strong>: We provide a simple and lightweight demo for FP8 and BF16 inference.</li>
<li><strong>SGLang</strong>: Fully support the DeepSeek-V3 model in both BF16 and FP8 inference modes, with Multi-Token Prediction <a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/issues/2591" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/sgl-project/sglang/issues/2591/hovercard">coming soon</a>.</li>
<li><strong>LMDeploy</strong>: Enables efficient FP8 and BF16 inference for local and cloud deployment.</li>
<li><strong>TensorRT-LLM</strong>: Currently supports BF16 inference and INT4/8 quantization, with FP8 support coming soon.</li>
<li><strong>vLLM</strong>: Support DeepSeek-V3 model with FP8 and BF16 modes for tensor parallelism and pipeline parallelism.</li>
<li><strong>LightLLM</strong>: Supports efficient single-node or multi-node deployment for FP8 and BF16.</li>
<li><strong>AMD GPU</strong>: Enables running the DeepSeek-V3 model on AMD GPUs via SGLang in both BF16 and FP8 modes.</li>
<li><strong>Huawei Ascend NPU</strong>: Supports running DeepSeek-V3 on Huawei Ascend devices in both INT8 and BF16.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Since FP8 training is natively adopted in our framework, we only provide FP8 weights. If you require BF16 weights for experimentation, you can use the provided conversion script to perform the transformation.</p>
<p dir="auto">Here is an example of converting FP8 weights to BF16:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cd inference
python fp8_cast_bf16.py --input-fp8-hf-path /path/to/fp8_weights --output-bf16-hf-path /path/to/bf16_weights"><pre><span class="pl-c1">cd</span> inference
python fp8_cast_bf16.py --input-fp8-hf-path /path/to/fp8_weights --output-bf16-hf-path /path/to/bf16_weights</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">Hugging Face's Transformers has not been directly supported yet.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.1 Inference with DeepSeek-Infer Demo (example only)</h3><a id="user-content-61-inference-with-deepseek-infer-demo-example-only" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.1 Inference with DeepSeek-Infer Demo (example only)" href="#61-inference-with-deepseek-infer-demo-example-only"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">System Requirements</h4><a id="user-content-system-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: System Requirements" href="#system-requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">Linux with Python 3.10 only. Mac and Windows are not supported.</p>
</div>
<p dir="auto">Dependencies:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-pip-requirements notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="torch==2.4.1
triton==3.0.0
transformers==4.46.3
safetensors==0.4.5"><pre><span class="pl-en">torch</span><span class="pl-k">==</span><span class="pl-c1">2.4.1</span>
<span class="pl-en">triton</span><span class="pl-k">==</span><span class="pl-c1">3.0.0</span>
<span class="pl-en">transformers</span><span class="pl-k">==</span><span class="pl-c1">4.46.3</span>
<span class="pl-en">safetensors</span><span class="pl-k">==</span><span class="pl-c1">0.4.5</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Model Weights &amp; Demo Code Preparation</h4><a id="user-content-model-weights--demo-code-preparation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Model Weights &amp; Demo Code Preparation" href="#model-weights--demo-code-preparation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">First, clone our DeepSeek-V3 GitHub repository:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3.git"><pre>git clone https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3.git</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Navigate to the <code>inference</code> folder and install dependencies listed in <code>requirements.txt</code>. Easiest way is to use a package manager like <code>conda</code> or <code>uv</code> to create a new virtual environment and install the dependencies.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cd DeepSeek-V3/inference
pip install -r requirements.txt"><pre><span class="pl-c1">cd</span> DeepSeek-V3/inference
pip install -r requirements.txt</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Download the model weights from Hugging Face, and put them into <code>/path/to/DeepSeek-V3</code> folder.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Model Weights Conversion</h4><a id="user-content-model-weights-conversion" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Model Weights Conversion" href="#model-weights-conversion"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Convert Hugging Face model weights to a specific format:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python convert.py --hf-ckpt-path /path/to/DeepSeek-V3 --save-path /path/to/DeepSeek-V3-Demo --n-experts 256 --model-parallel 16"><pre>python convert.py --hf-ckpt-path /path/to/DeepSeek-V3 --save-path /path/to/DeepSeek-V3-Demo --n-experts 256 --model-parallel 16</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run</h4><a id="user-content-run" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run" href="#run"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Then you can chat with DeepSeek-V3:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="torchrun --nnodes 2 --nproc-per-node 8 --node-rank $RANK --master-addr $ADDR generate.py --ckpt-path /path/to/DeepSeek-V3-Demo --config configs/config_671B.json --interactive --temperature 0.7 --max-new-tokens 200"><pre>torchrun --nnodes 2 --nproc-per-node 8 --node-rank <span class="pl-smi">$RANK</span> --master-addr <span class="pl-smi">$ADDR</span> generate.py --ckpt-path /path/to/DeepSeek-V3-Demo --config configs/config_671B.json --interactive --temperature 0.7 --max-new-tokens 200</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or batch inference on a given file:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="torchrun --nnodes 2 --nproc-per-node 8 --node-rank $RANK --master-addr $ADDR generate.py --ckpt-path /path/to/DeepSeek-V3-Demo --config configs/config_671B.json --input-file $FILE"><pre>torchrun --nnodes 2 --nproc-per-node 8 --node-rank <span class="pl-smi">$RANK</span> --master-addr <span class="pl-smi">$ADDR</span> generate.py --ckpt-path /path/to/DeepSeek-V3-Demo --config configs/config_671B.json --input-file <span class="pl-smi">$FILE</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.2 Inference with SGLang (recommended)</h3><a id="user-content-62-inference-with-sglang-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.2 Inference with SGLang (recommended)" href="#62-inference-with-sglang-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang">SGLang</a> currently supports <a href="https://lmsys.org/blog/2024-09-04-sglang-v0-3/#deepseek-multi-head-latent-attention-mla-throughput-optimizations" rel="nofollow">MLA optimizations</a>, <a href="https://lmsys.org/blog/2024-12-04-sglang-v0-4/#data-parallelism-attention-for-deepseek-models" rel="nofollow">DP Attention</a>, FP8 (W8A8), FP8 KV Cache, and Torch Compile, delivering state-of-the-art latency and throughput performance among open-source frameworks.</p>
<p dir="auto">Notably, <a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/releases/tag/v0.4.1">SGLang v0.4.1</a> fully supports running DeepSeek-V3 on both <strong>NVIDIA and AMD GPUs</strong>, making it a highly versatile and robust solution.</p>
<p dir="auto">SGLang also supports <a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/tree/main/benchmark/deepseek_v3#example-serving-with-2-h208">multi-node tensor parallelism</a>, enabling you to run this model on multiple network-connected machines.</p>
<p dir="auto">Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) is in development, and progress can be tracked in the <a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/issues/2591" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/sgl-project/sglang/issues/2591/hovercard">optimization plan</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Here are the launch instructions from the SGLang team: <a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/tree/main/benchmark/deepseek_v3">https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/tree/main/benchmark/deepseek_v3</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.3 Inference with LMDeploy (recommended)</h3><a id="user-content-63-inference-with-lmdeploy-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.3 Inference with LMDeploy (recommended)" href="#63-inference-with-lmdeploy-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/InternLM/lmdeploy">LMDeploy</a>, a flexible and high-performance inference and serving framework tailored for large language models, now supports DeepSeek-V3. It offers both offline pipeline processing and online deployment capabilities, seamlessly integrating with PyTorch-based workflows.</p>
<p dir="auto">For comprehensive step-by-step instructions on running DeepSeek-V3 with LMDeploy, please refer to here: <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="2759498277" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/InternLM/lmdeploy/issues/2960" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/InternLM/lmdeploy/issues/2960/hovercard" href="https://github.com/InternLM/lmdeploy/issues/2960">InternLM/lmdeploy#2960</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.4 Inference with TRT-LLM (recommended)</h3><a id="user-content-64-inference-with-trt-llm-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.4 Inference with TRT-LLM (recommended)" href="#64-inference-with-trt-llm-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM">TensorRT-LLM</a> now supports the DeepSeek-V3 model, offering precision options such as BF16 and INT4/INT8 weight-only. Support for FP8 is currently in progress and will be released soon. You can access the custom branch of TRTLLM specifically for DeepSeek-V3 support through the following link to experience the new features directly: <a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/tree/main/examples/deepseek_v3">https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/tree/main/examples/deepseek_v3</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.5 Inference with vLLM (recommended)</h3><a id="user-content-65-inference-with-vllm-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.5 Inference with vLLM (recommended)" href="#65-inference-with-vllm-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm">vLLM</a> v0.6.6 supports DeepSeek-V3 inference for FP8 and BF16 modes on both NVIDIA and AMD GPUs. Aside from standard techniques, vLLM offers <em>pipeline parallelism</em> allowing you to run this model on multiple machines connected by networks. For detailed guidance, please refer to the <a href="https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/serving/distributed_serving.html" rel="nofollow">vLLM instructions</a>. Please feel free to follow <a href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/11539" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/issues/11539/hovercard">the enhancement plan</a> as well.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.6 Inference with LightLLM (recommended)</h3><a id="user-content-66-inference-with-lightllm-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.6 Inference with LightLLM (recommended)" href="#66-inference-with-lightllm-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/ModelTC/lightllm/tree/main">LightLLM</a> v1.0.1 supports single-machine and multi-machine tensor parallel deployment for DeepSeek-R1 (FP8/BF16) and provides mixed-precision deployment, with more quantization modes continuously integrated. For more details, please refer to <a href="https://lightllm-en.readthedocs.io/en/latest/getting_started/quickstart.html" rel="nofollow">LightLLM instructions</a>. Additionally, LightLLM offers PD-disaggregation deployment for DeepSeek-V2, and the implementation of PD-disaggregation for DeepSeek-V3 is in development.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.7 Recommended Inference Functionality with AMD GPUs</h3><a id="user-content-67-recommended-inference-functionality-with-amd-gpus" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.7 Recommended Inference Functionality with AMD GPUs" href="#67-recommended-inference-functionality-with-amd-gpus"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In collaboration with the AMD team, we have achieved Day-One support for AMD GPUs using SGLang, with full compatibility for both FP8 and BF16 precision. For detailed guidance, please refer to the <a href="#63-inference-with-lmdeploy-recommended">SGLang instructions</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.8 Recommended Inference Functionality with Huawei Ascend NPUs</h3><a id="user-content-68-recommended-inference-functionality-with-huawei-ascend-npus" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.8 Recommended Inference Functionality with Huawei Ascend NPUs" href="#68-recommended-inference-functionality-with-huawei-ascend-npus"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="https://www.hiascend.com/en/software/mindie" rel="nofollow">MindIE</a> framework from the Huawei Ascend community has successfully adapted the BF16 version of DeepSeek-V3. For step-by-step guidance on Ascend NPUs, please follow the <a href="https://modelers.cn/models/MindIE/deepseekv3" rel="nofollow">instructions here</a>.</p>
